A real PMS LOCALISES its own text: `X-Plex-Language` (header or query) beats `Accept-Language`, a regional form works like its primary subtag, an unknown language falls back to English, 28 languages answer differently, and the filter/sort menus, `Meta` type titles, hub titles, a GENERATED `Season N`, a stream's language name and `displayTitle` and all 200+ `/:/prefs` strings move while the LIBRARY's own rows, `All Movies`, an axis's `title2` and `Unplayed` do not -- the table is keyed by ATTRIBUTE and then by the English string (`translations.json`).
A real PMS ROUTES before it authenticates: with no token an unknown path is a 404, exactly eleven single-segment paths are 401 (`library hubs playlists devices butler servers clients accounts activities playQueues log`), `/identity` and `/security` are 200 and every other one is a 403; `GET /` is a 401 with a 193-byte body that walks a browser into the web client.
Every response names its connection state (`Connection: Keep-Alive` + `Keep-Alive: timeout=20`, and an idle socket really is closed at 20 s); a paged container answers `X-Plex-Container-Start`/`-Total-Size` as HEADERS and the paging HEADER beats the query per parameter (a header `Start=abc` beside a valid query is a 500); `Range` applies to EVERY response body (206/416, an unrecognised unit read as bytes, a 206 never gzipped); a malformed request header is a 400 (no colon, a TAB in a value, a name over 1024, a value over 4096); the Accept `q` grammar RANKS a zero rather than dropping it (a range qualifies only if its type is exactly `application/json`/`application/xml` and it has no parameters or a first parameter beginning exactly `q=`; no parameter weighs 1.0, a single parseable non-negative q weighs that, everything else weighs 0 and still qualifies); `Accept-Encoding` tokens match exactly and case-sensitively (`GZIP`, `x-gzip` and `*` get plain; `gzip;q=0` is still gzipped); no `100 Continue`; a preflight with no `Origin` carries no CORS headers; `/web` and `/web/` are a `302 Moved Temporarily` with an ABSOLUTE Location from the request's own `Host` and `Cache-Control: public` (the one non-`no-cache` place on the surface) while every `/web/*` asset is `no-cache`, `Accept-Ranges: bytes`, never gzipped, `.js` is `text/javascript`, `.html` carries no charset and `.ico` is `application/octet-stream`; a section's bare `/composite` is a 404 and only `/composite/{version}` answers; `X-Plex-Platform: Roku` strips `id` and `Pivot` from every library `Directory` of `/media/providers`; a `Meta/Type` orders its children `Filter, Sort, Field`, `includeDetails=1` carries no `active`/`activeDirection` on a Sort nor `advanced` on a Filter where `includeMeta=1` carries all three, the rows `includeChildren=1` inlines carry no librarySection triple, and a MediaContainer's attributes come out `size`, then `totalSize`/`offset` when paged, then alphabetically; `POST /library/metadata/{id}/extras` is a 400 whatever the body; **a real PMS sends no `Server:` banner at all**.
`/playlists/{id}/items` is a real typed QUERY (`type=2` answers the SHOW owning the member episodes, `type=3` their seasons, `-1` everything, `1.5` a 400; the library filters apply; `sort=`/`limit=` are parsed then ignored and an unparseable one is a 500) and `?includeMeta=1` carries the page's own tab bar; a playlist is born `viewCount: 1` with `lastViewedAt == addedAt` and the pair is on the LISTING row only; `/playlists` filters by `title`, `smart`, `sectionID` and `type` (15; `type=abc` is a 500) and its default order is SMART first then `titleSort`; `sort=mediaCount` is real there and `leafCount` a 500; `POST /playlists/{id}/items` is a 404 (only PUT adds); an empty `title=` is stored verbatim; `smart=1` over a comma-joined KEY LIST is a 400 (the test is the uri's SHAPE, never whether it resolves); a uri the server cannot resolve adds NOTHING; `PUT /playlists/{id}` takes the whole ITEM edit grammar (thumb/art mint artwork under `/library/metadata/{playlist ratingKey}/`, every `.locked` locks, `year`/`originallyAvailableAt`/`studio` store, `userRating`/`guid`/`duration`/timestamps are accepted and discarded); `playlistItemID` is a SERVER-WIDE sequence and a smart list's items carry none (every item write on one is a 400); a playlist's artwork lives under `/library/metadata/{ratingKey}/` (posters, arts, thumbs, banners, tree all 200) while `/playlists/{id}/{posters,arts,thumb,art}` and the bare `composite` are 404s.
A shuffled play queue is really SHUFFLED (plexsim reversed it deterministically); `includeBefore`/`includeAfter` window the queue with NO `window=` (unlimited radius) and each is a boolean whose every other value is a 400; EVERY item delete answers the container as it stood BEFORE it and only the SELECTED one also drops the rows; a move refuses `after=<itself>` (404) and an empty `after=` (400); a queue built from a PLAYLIST uri carries `playQueuePlaylistID`/`playQueuePlaylistTitle` and no `playQueueSourceURI`.
The SECTION save path stores an unlocked `titleSort.value=` until the next TITLE write re-derives it (where the ITEM path discards it); a show's `contentRating` reaches only the episodes carrying none of their own; a show's `index` cascades onto its seasons' `parentIndex`; a season that LOSES its year gains `parentYear` in the same read; `type=abc` on the save path is a 400; an UNMATCHED item drops its agent-driven prefs (a show loses `languageOverride` and `useOriginalTitle`, a MOVIE loses the whole `Preferences` block, a season keeps its three); and on the section save path the `state 3` frame carries the row's title from BEFORE the write while `state 5` carries the new one.

Cycle 17 (29 findings, all landed on `main` at b3e3ea7 -- **do not re-report any of these**):
Three fixtures were captured off the WRONG VERSION and are now split per version: `Meta.Type[].Field` (1.42.2 advertises 34 movie Fields and 20 episode ones against 1.43.4's 40 and 26, differing in exactly the six media-codec axes `hdr10plus`/`atmos`/`videoCodec`/`audioCodec`/`subtitleCodec`/`audioLayout`), and those six are UNKNOWN fields on 1.42.2 so a listing filtered on one answers EVERY row and `/query/parse` reports an `Error`; `system_agents.json` (`tv.plex.agents.nfo.movie` and `.series` are 1.43.4-only -- 1.42.2 lists four modern agents and two per media type); and `translations.json`, widened from 342 to 451 entries over ~100 paths (the whole library and item PREFERENCES family's `label`/`summary`/`enumValues`, the seventeen search hubs, the butler's task titles and descriptions, a generated `Episode N`, `/query/parse`'s `fieldTitle`).
The localiser reaches INSIDE a row for exactly two blocks: the `Preferences` block's `label`/`summary`/`enumValues` and a `Stream`'s language fields -- where the two display titles are COMPOSED from the localised name and the omission is decided on the ENGLISH one -- while the row's own title, summary and tags stay English.
A real PMS escapes XML attribute values into pure ASCII: `&amp;`/`&lt;`/`&gt;` named, `"` and `'` NUMERIC (`&#34;`, `&#39;`), TAB decimal `&#9;` but NEWLINE and CR HEX (`&#xA;`, `&#xD;`), every codepoint from U+0080 up its DECIMAL reference (an emoji is one `&#128512;`), every control XML 1.0 forbids a literal `?`; the surface carries no element TEXT.
A ROW's XML attribute ORDER is measured -- 25 element buckets in `xmlout._ATTR_ORDER`, a topological sort of every PAIR both references emit, 634 inverted pairs fixed and 0 left; a tag is `id, filter, tag`, a SORT row `default, defaultDirection, descKey, firstCharacterKey, key, title`, a `firstCharacter` bucket `size, key, title`.
`titleSort` strips a leading run of PUNCTUATION as well as whitespace, before the article: in ASCII only `" ' ( - . [ { ~`, and outside it every codepoint from U+0080..U+00BF and U+2000..U+2FFF, while U+0100..U+1FFF and U+3000 up stay; the run may mix with whitespace and repeat, and a title made of nothing else keeps its own text.
A show's `/allLeaves` -- and no other container -- stamps a leaf's BORROWED artwork (`grandparentThumb`, `grandparentArt`, `grandparentTheme`, `art`) with the LEAF's own `addedAt`; everywhere else they carry the show's current version. A property of the LIST, not of the row.
A save a real PMS REFUSES writes NOTHING (validate the whole form before applying any of it); the ITEM edit path's 400 means "this request edits nothing" -- with a real edit beside it every unrecognised name is IGNORED and each alone is a 400 -- while the SECTION path answers 200 to a form that writes nothing and still 400s an unknown `.value`.
A collection's `/children` on the METADATA path is the LIGHT grid row with the librarySection triple on the CONTAINER, `allowSync: true` and no `thumb`; the collection ITEM paths take one verb each (`/items` PUT plus GET/HEAD, `/items/{rk}` DELETE, `/move` PUT) and 404 the rest, a non-numeric ratingKey on the DELETE is a 404 where an unknown number is a 200, and the MOVE refuses `after=<itself>`/empty/unparseable with a 400, sends an `after=` naming no member to the FRONT and works through the `/children/{rk}/move` alias; a comma-joined add TOLERATES the members it cannot use and only a single-key uri of the wrong type is a 400.
A LOCAL artwork candidate names a server path: `ratingKey` is the bare uri and `key`/`thumb` are `/library/metadata/{id}/file?url=<encoded, `.` as `%2E`>` which really serves the image; a `media://` or `upload://` candidate carries no `provider`; `DELETE .../thumb` clears the poster like `/poster`; an artwork UPLOAD moves `updatedAt` onto the new stamp exactly as a PICK does.
A library PREFERENCE write moves the SECTION's `updatedAt`; an INT pref that does not parse is stored as 0 (not ignored); a TEXT pref is stored percent-escaped for `:` and `|` (lowercase hex) with a value carrying `%` stored EMPTY.
Twelve butler tasks report a different sentence than plexsim did, three report SEVERAL and five report one where it reported none; `CleanOldBundles` is titled `Cleaning Bundles` and steps its activity SUBTITLE through four named phases, `OptimizeDatabase` is `Optimizing database`/`Optimizing Database`; and an ACTIVITY's subtitle is the row's FRAME title (a season's `<show> S01`, an episode's `<show> S01 E01`), not `title (year)`.
`POST` on an item ACTION leaf is a 404 (`/refresh`, `/analyze`, `/unmatch` take PUT) while `POST /library/sections/{id}/refresh` is a 200; `/match` answers EVERY verb with its no-parameters 400; `?force=` on a section refresh is PARSED (`abc`, `1.5`, empty are 400s); a KNOWN single-segment path (`/butler`, `/devices`) falls through to the legacy plug-in dispatcher for the verbs it does not implement; and `GET /livetv` is that dispatcher's three-row container (`dvrs`, `epg`, `sessions`, `content="plugins"`), not an empty one.

Cycle 18 (26 findings, all landed on `main` at b3e3ea7 -- **do not re-report any of these**):
A title the SERVER generated is stored NOWHERE: a season's `Season N` and an unmatched episode's `Episode N` have an EMPTY stored sort title (`titleSort=` answers 740 of zen's 741 seasons and 4,630 of its 9,287 episodes, `titleSort<<=0` the same set, every LETTER bucket of a season `firstCharacter` listing answers none while `#` answers them all), and it is in no search index either (`search?type=3&query=season` answers nothing until the season is renamed; zen's `type=3&title=Season` is 0 of 741). An agent-supplied `Season 1`/`Episode 1` IS stored and nothing on the row tells the two apart.
Eleven Latin-1 characters fold in `titleSort` that NFD cannot reach, swept character by character over U+0080..U+02FF: `AE->E D->D x->x O->0 P->P ss->s ae->e eth->O div->/ oslash->0 thorn->p` (`O`/`o` slash become the DIGIT ZERO and eth a capital `O`). Nothing above U+02FF folds.
The bare `title=` matcher also reads the row's ORIGINAL title, and only it does: `title=the` is 1,484 of zen's 3,887 where the title alone explains 1,481. `title!=`, `==`, `!==`, `<=` and `>=` read the title alone.
On an UNLISTED string field (`titleSort`, `summary`, `tagline`, `originalTitle`) the four ordering keys really ORDER: `<=` is `<<=` and `>=` is `>>=`, case-insensitive lexicographic, both STRICT. They are begins-with/ends-with only on an ADVERTISED string field.
`/library/sections/{id}/search?type=N` types its container from the ASKED type, not the library: `type=3` is `viewGroup=season` and `viewMode` 65593, `type=4` is `viewGroup=episode` with `mixedParents`, and a MOVIE library answers both. The section GRID keeps the library's own type.
`All <library title>` on a movie library and the constant `All Shows` on a show one, in both the `/all` container's `title2` and the `all` pivot row. Not localised.
Three endpoints, three meanings for `limit=`: `/library/recentlyAdded?limit=N` caps AND rewrites `totalSize` (0 none, negative lifts the 50-row cap, unparseable ignored); `/status/sessions/history/all` caps and `-1` answers NONE; a section listing's `limit=0` answers ONE row and an unparseable one is a 500. `group=` on the history page is a 400.
`parentIndex=` and an out-of-enum `resolution=` refuse on `/library/all` and `/playlists` as well as on a section listing.
`/hubs`, and a SHOW library's `/hubs/sections/{id}`, 500 fifteen filter keys (`duration lastViewedAt lastRatedAt viewCount skipCount viewOffset userRating unwatched resolution hdr mediaSize file parentIndex slug group`) for any non-empty value and whatever operator; an EMPTY value and an unknown field are 200s, and a MOVIE library's hub page takes all fifteen.
`/system` PAGES (`totalSize` FIRST, no `offset`, and the response headers) where `/system/scanners/{N}` ignores paging; the plug-in framework's container is ordered `[totalSize,] noHistory, replaceParent, size, identifier`; `/system/search` is a real empty container carrying `prefsKey`; and the whole `/system` subtree has its own envelope -- PATCH is a 405 (the only one on the surface), a bare OPTIONS an empty 200, every other verb an EMPTY-bodied 404.
Four more single-segment paths are the plug-in dispatcher's POPULATED listings (`/media`, `/services`, `/status`, `/statistics`) and `/security` is a real empty container with NO `content` key; a KNOWN single-segment path answers the dispatcher for every verb it does not implement, `PUT /identity` and `PUT /hubs` included.
The playlist item paths take one verb each: `/playlists/{id}` and `/items` GET/HEAD/PUT/DELETE, `/items/{iid}` DELETE (PUT is a 400, a non-numeric id a 404), `/items/{iid}/move` PUT. `DELETE /library/metadata/{playlist}` is an empty 200 that deletes NOTHING where `DELETE /playlists/{id}` is a 204 that does.
A repeated `id=` on the section save path edits nothing (the comma list edits both); `POST` on `/split` and `/merge` is a 404; a write to `/library/parts/{id}` resolves the part FIRST (an unknown or malformed id is a 404 whatever the parameters); `/subtitles` answers 404 to DELETE, PATCH and OPTIONS.
A library DELETE pushes one `state 9 deleted` frame per row -- `type: 12`, an EMPTY title, `sectionID` the NEGATIVE section id -- between the 10% and 40% activity updates.
`section_prefs_new.json` is per VERSION too (the fourth such fixture): on 1.42.2 the AGENT's settings are appended whatever the `type=`, and 1.43.4 adds five generic rows to `tv.plex.agents.none` at types 1 and 2.
An item's `audioLanguage`/`subtitleLanguage` enum is led by the ITEM's own stream languages (bare 639-1 tags, audio first then subtitles, each named in its own language) before the generic regional list.

Cycle 19 (31 findings, all landed on `main` at b3e3ea7 -- **do not re-report any of these**):
A BOOLEAN filter answers the true set to exactly two requests -- `field=1` and `field!=0`; every other operator and every other value answers the rows where the column is FALSE (zen's `unwatched=1` is 3,866 of 3,887 and `!=1`, `==1`, `<=1`, `>=1`, `<<=1`, `>>=1`, `=0`, `==0`, `!==0`, `=2`, `!=2`, `=`, `=01`, `=true` are all the same 21).
A row with NO value for a numeric column matches NOTHING, `!=` and `!==` included (zen's 742 rated movies split 368/374 across `rating<=7` and `rating>>=7`, so the 3,145 unrated are in neither); and `==`/`!==` there are an exact STRING comparison of the stored number (`rating==7` is 0 where `rating==7.0` is 6).
A `tag`-typed COLUMN takes all eight operators where a tag-TABLE field takes two: `contentRating`'s ordering keys are a BYTE-ordered comparison over the stored string, an absent value compares as `""`, `=` is case-SENSITIVE and `==` is not. `resolution` is the opposite -- every operator answers the `=` set, and it matches ANY of a row's Media.
The section `Meta`'s `active` follows the REQUEST, not the library (`?type=3` marks `season`, `?type=4` marks `episode`); `?includeDetails=1` honours `includeAdvanced=1` (9 sorts bare, 15 with it), carries no `folder` Type row and marks none active.
A row that HAS children names its child list -- `key="/library/metadata/{id}/children"` on every show and season of both references, where an episode keeps the bare item path.
`excludeFields=` strips the field from every CHILD ELEMENT (`id` off Media/Part/Stream/Genre/Guid, `file` off Part, `url` off Image, `value` off Rating); `excludeElements=Image` and `=UltraBlurColors` are NO-OPS on both references; `checkFiles=` is validated (`0`, `1`, `01` pass, everything else is a 400).
`/library/metadata/{id}/extras/all` is a real leaf, the same container `/extras` answers; only `/extras/{n}` is the 404.
Four more XML ordering rules: a `Meta`'s `Type` rows precede its `FieldType` rows; `Common`, `Feature` (`scrobbleKey` first), `Type` and the `/media/providers` library `Directory` are element buckets of their own; the rows of `/library/recentlyAdded` and `/library/onDeck` LEAD with `allowSync` and the librarySection triple; a LIGHT row puts `Guid` directly after `UltraBlurColors`, before its tags, where a detail row puts it after `Genre`/`Country`.
A real PMS's JSON key order IS its XML order -- the container's, the row's, `Media`'s, `Part`'s and the row's child-element keys. `xmlout._split` is now the one answer both writers ask.
On a SHOW or SEASON listing a media column is read from the LEAVES and matches when ANY leaf does (zen's `duration<=1200000` and `>=1200000` answer 39 and 483 of 505).
The default listing order IS `sort=titleSort` and every tie falls back to `titleSort` ASCENDING, in both directions -- re-measured against zen's own ordering of 3,887 real rows.
A library EDIT scans only when the form carries `language` or `scanner` (`agent` alone, with `name`, or with a `prefs[...]` is a 200 with ZERO frames) and it REFUSES a `type` naming a different type from the library's own. `?path=` SCOPES a scan: the section's own Location, an empty value and no path scan everything; a parent of it, a child of it and a path it does not hold scan nothing.
Three butler tasks push the wrong thing: `RefreshLibraries` runs a section SCAN per library; `OptimizeDatabase` starts at 0 with an EMPTY subtitle and steps 0/10/40/60 to an `ended` at 100; `CleanOldBundles` has SEVEN updates ending on two `Deleting N metadata bundles` steps at 25 and 50.
A show library's scan names the SHOW before its seasons, in a `progress` row with no activity update of its own; `provider.subscriptions.process` is the one `cancellable: true` activity; an analyze pushes NO `backgroundProcessingQueue` frame; `PUT /updater/check` pushes one `update.statechange` frame carrying an `AutoUpdateNotification` row.
`DELETE /devices/{id}` and `DELETE /accounts/{id}` are 404s for an id the server lists and one it does not; `PUT /activities/{x}` is a 400 where its DELETE is a 404; `HEAD /:/prefs` is a 200.
Four settings answer 403 to a PUT (`PlexOnlineUrl`, `TranscoderH264Options`, `TranscoderH264OptionsOverride`, `allowMediaDeletionLanOnly`) even when the value written is the one already stored, while `ButlerStartHour` and `ButlerEndHour` take a WORD with a 200 that stores nothing.
`server_prefs.json` is the FIFTH per-version fixture: `AllowLegacyAgents` and `CLDriver` are 1.43.4-only, and a write to one is refused as an unknown preference.
The Add Library form refuses a missing or unlisted `language` (the agent's own list, case-insensitive), accepts an EMPTY `location` and accepts a NUMERIC `type`, creating a library of that type.
`filters=` on a notification channel is a WHITELIST and `all` is not an event type, so a socket opened with `filters=all` receives NOTHING. Always open with a control write.

## Measured and deliberately NOT implemented — do not re-report

ICU collation; image filters; random: sort regeneration; updatedAt/contentChangedAt semantics; session ordering and the Session block; duplicate collection titles; scan refreshing observability; transcoderVideo* fields; XML-only /system responses; text/plain error bodies; Part exists/accessible on metadata reads; /livetv/epg countries/languages; legacy agents and the /:/plugins listing; typo-tolerant search; season/episode external Guids (no data); Common's episode/season quirks; the random actor/director shelf; markers and chapters (no data); /services/browse mount points; mp4 optimizedForStreaming; the eventsource transport (chunked vs Connection: close); download-queue item adds; a valid transcode start (plexsim has no encoder); anything on plex.tv (Optimize, Sync, the plug-in store, the account side). Cycle 7 additions: the `count=1` hub quirk on kids (it drops Recently Watched; fun does not); orphaned collection tags carrying a `guid`; /system/agents' legacy primary/MediaType shape (the modern rows match); /library/metadata/{id}/matches?manual=1 candidates; thumbBlurHash/artBlurHash; `slug=` 500 on listings (the `guid=` prefix was implemented in cycle 8); /squareArts (200 on 1.43.4, 404 on 1.42.2, and plexsim reports 1.42.2). Cycle 8 additions: fun's `newest` bound (47/60 rows, no visible rule); fun's Recently Viewed order; ICU numeric collation in autocomplete order; All episodes at Start>0 (kids drops, fun keeps); `guid==`/`guid!=`; untyped /library/all with `show.guid`; /system/services/url/lookup's plug-in traceback; mixed folder levels; fun's postplay `movie.extras` and `tv.ondeck` shelves and related `movie.same.actor.N`; child elements outside the measured order list; plexsim-minted studio tag ids. Cycle 9: ultrablur and /colors; /file?url=; episode allLeaves container disagreements; kids history account filtering; genre/actor /hubs/items identifiers; /library/matches provider options; paging on write-shaped reads; plexscratch instrument limits (session-bound timelines, no-duration timelines); optimize, showOrdering and library-create frames captured only in part; reference tag-column oddities and merge?ids=<self>; plexscratch item DELETE 400 and editionTitle 403.

Cycle 10 batch 1 additions: what the probe cannot give (`refFrames`; a Matroska video stream's bitrate; DTS-HD, TrueHD, FLAC, Opus and subtitle bitrates; `headerCompression`; Part `packetLength`; `hasThumbnail`, `indexes`, the `failureBIF*` fields; `eia_608` captions); which audio track is `selected` (it follows the account's language settings); the counterexamples E-AC-3-with-Atmos in mp4, DTS Express with no channel layout and TrueHD in mp4 with no bit depth; 1.43.4-only labelling (an untagged track called English, `lc` and the mono layout omitted on AAC clips, Media bitrates ~8 kbps under the probe's). Cycle 10 batch 2 additions: what the copy cannot do (the download queue with no account, `POST .../posters?url=` to an external image, a provider match search, `POST /library/metadata/{id}/subtitles` answering 500 with nothing in its log); captured and not reproduced (a library create's per-item `created` frames, the `backgroundProcessingQueue` frame a minute later, the 201 row's `metadataAgentProviderGroupId`/`uuid`/`createdAt`/`contentChangedAt: -1`, the `provider.subscriptions.process` activity pairs a scan ends with); copy oddities (the artwork write's response body and the row's `thumb` differing by the copy's timezone offset, a smart collection's stale `childCount`). Cycle 10 batch 3 additions: an unclaimed copy 401s `/status/sessions/terminate` in every form and 404s `/statistics/*`, so neither is measurable there; only Plex Web was ever seen, so `Player.profile` is `Web` for every session; an unmatched row's timeline answer carries no `skipCount`/`viewCount`/`viewOffset`; `/statistics/resources` per-second host samples.

Cycle 11 additions: `/status/sessions/history/all` answering `size 0` on BOTH kids and zen while single reads work (not a kids oddity, and not reproduced); `includeBandwidths=1` (`deepAnalysisVersion`, `requiredBandwidths` -- needs Plex's own deep analysis); `/library/people/{id}` answering any TAG id (kids' id 1 is a `mediaProcessingTarget`, tagType 42, rendering as `<Tag>` with MediaSettings and Device children); `playQueueLastAddedItemID` re-anchoring on a MOVE (seen, not pinned); the subtitle SEARCH path answering 503 on the isolated copy (needs OpenSubtitles); the `<CODEC> External` subtitle label having only one witness.

Refuted by re-measurement in cycle 10 — do not re-report: the `Session` block appearing only after a DECISION (re-measured with a decision that answers 200, the copy carries no `Session` block at all).

Refuted by re-measurement in cycle 9 — do not re-report: progress state=playing opening a session; a bogus playQueueItemID selecting by ratingKey; a library type change being a 400; a collection create with no uri being a 400; smart collections tagging members; a whole-library playlist add adding nothing; continuous=1 stopping at one episode; an unknown playlist move `after` being a no-op (it goes to the front).
Refuted by re-measurement in cycle 8 — do not re-report: "a yearless season in Recently Added lacks year/titleSort/updatedAt" is cycle 7's season-year rule, not a new one.
Refuted by re-measurement in cycle 7 — do not re-report: "/system/agents has the wrong shape" (false for the modern agents); "`title!=` is the negation of the word match" (`!=` is still a substring); "a double space never matches" (empty words are allowed).

Cycle 12 additions -- do not re-report: the tag `count` on a DETAIL row (**refuted outright**: neither server sends `count` on a detail row's tags; it exists only on the tag `Directory` rows of `/hubs/search` and `/library/search`, where plexsim already emits it with the right value); a library create's per-item frames over real files and the `backgroundProcessingQueue` regeneration; the 201 row's `metadataAgentProviderGroupId`, `updatedAt`, `createdAt` and `contentChangedAt: -1`; the download queue (404 everywhere on the copy, and Plex Web never builds the path); the subtitle SEARCH and the apply-a-result PUT (503 -- no OpenSubtitles from an isolated copy); `/search` ignoring `limit` entirely; `titleSort` article stripping beyond the/a/an (agent-supplied, and the samples are far too small to infer a rule from).

Differences that are purely CATALOG CONTENT (different titles, counts, ratingKeys, artwork URLs, dates, data plexsim's catalog does not have) are not findings. **fun is OUT OF SCOPE: send it nothing, do not ssh to it, do not read it.** The two witnesses are **kids (Plex 1.43.4)** and **zen (Plex 1.42.2 -- the exact build plexsim reports)**. A rule is a finding when BOTH answer it; a field only kids sends is a version difference and not a finding, because plexsim reports 1.42.2. Say for every rule which servers you measured it on.

Cycle 13 additions — do not re-report: the score PROMOTION `/hubs/search` applies when a query has fuzzy hits (a hub whose best row scores above ~0.5 moves to the front; four of five zen queries fit and `comedy` also demotes `episode` from 4th to 13th, which nothing predicts — the base order is implemented, the promotion is not); the codec axes' empty-value behaviour (kids nothing, zen every row — a version difference, not a rule); and season 0 / specials, which neither reference holds.

Cycle 14: the three cascades re-measured and NOT built (the show/season REFRESH sequence, `analyze`'s per-LEAF frames plus its chapter-thumbs activity, and the 74-frame library create with `queueRegenerated` at +60.8 s) -- the DRIVER is implementing these three this cycle, so do not report them; the episode SPLIT (the copy cannot re-identify offline, so it is not comparable with cycle 4's online measurement); `ratingCount` on a collection row; and the non-English article strip in `titleSort`, which is per-item LANGUAGE and not mechanical (Spanish `La` and German `Das` are stripped on zen while `Les`, `Die` and `O` are not).

**Cycle 16:** a ROW's XML attribute order (the CONTAINER's is settled; a tag row is `id, filter, tag[, role, thumb]`, which is not alphabetical, and the rest was never mapped -- **this one is territory A's this cycle, so measure it, do not skip it**); `Set-Cookie: com.plexapp.system=...` on `/system` and `/system/agents/contributors` (the plug-in framework's); the 1024/4096/~41 KB header limits as exact constants (measured as brackets, not bisected); the XML half of the localisation language by language (it follows by construction, but was never diffed); `/library/metadata/{playlist}`'s container attributes (`allowSync`, `identifier`, `mediaTagPrefix`, `mediaTagVersion`, measured on the copy); and the reference's own duplicated `Content-Length` on a 206, which is a quirk rather than a rule.

**Cycle 17 additions -- do not re-report:** `PUT /library/metadata/{id}/poster?url=nonsense` being a 404 (**REFUTED** -- both sides answer 200 and re-version, twice in a row with a distinct stamp each time); an EPISODE refresh running the SHOW's activity (two runs of the same request on the same copy disagreed, so only the SUBTITLE half is built); a `state 2` / `metadataState "matching"` opener on a season or episode refresh (reported by the hunt, not reproduced in either driver capture); a remote artwork candidate's `images.plex.tv` proxy `thumb` (plex.tv is out of scope); the three legacy `com.plexapp.agents.*` rows at `/system/agents` (no plug-in framework); the non-alphanumeric `firstCharacter` bucket, re-read against zen's section 2 and still single-witness; and the butler's per-task COUNTS, whose templates are measured verbatim while the numbers are that server's own content.

**Cycle 18:** `/playlists/{id}` resolving any metadata ratingKey (both references answer 200 with that item in a bare `{size: 1}` container and 500 on its `/items`, but the projection is not stably characterised between them and no client builds the path); the `/system` 404's MISSING `Content-Type` (the status and the empty body are reproduced, the absent header is not); the library delete's frame IDS (the server's own internal metadata ids, 14 for a nine-row library, with nothing in the response to explain the id space); and the eleven-character fold table's MECHANISM (the targets are measured, and nothing explains why the o-slash becomes a digit).

## Your territory: the SHOW library at TYPE 3 and TYPE 4 -- the season row and the episode row, against 741 real seasons and 9,287 real episodes

Every mechanical sweep this project has run has produced a batch of findings no amount of re-hunting the previous dimension would have. The last two cycles' best instrument was **holding plexsim's own engine out against a real library's rows**: cycle 18 ran `filters.compare` over zen's 3,887 MOVIE rows and found the `title=`/`originalTitle` rule; cycle 19 pointed the same tool at its 507 SHOW rows and it disagreed **74 times**, and behind those disagreements were four rules about the filter grammar that eighteen cycles of endpoint-by-endpoint measurement had never reached -- because each one needs a library where most rows have NO value for the column being filtered.

**Cycle 19's holdout ran at `type=2` only.** zen's section 2 also holds **741 seasons** and **9,287 episodes**, and the SEASON row is the row with the most DERIVED fields on it in the whole surface: `parentTitle`, `parentStudio`, `parentYear`, `parentGuid`, `parentThumb`, `parentIndex`, `grandparent*` on its leaves, a generated `Season N` title, a borrowed `art`/`thumb`, `leafCount`/`viewedLeafCount`, and its own year-or-the-show's. Nearly every one of those is a rule this project derived from a five-clip library with ONE show in it.

Do this in four parts and report each part's coverage numbers whether or not it produced a finding.

**Part 1 -- the season row and the episode row, field by field, at scale.** Fetch zen's seasons and episodes in pages (always send `X-Plex-Container-Start`/`-Size`) and census every attribute that appears on any row, with how many of the rows carry it and the distribution of its values. Then ask, for each derived field, whether plexsim's rule reproduces it: does a season's `parentYear` really appear only when the season has no year of its own; is `titleSort` on a generated `Season N` really absent on all 741; is `parentGuid` the SEASON's guid on an episode and the SHOW's on a season, on all 9,287; does an episode carry its show's `contentRating` only when it has none of its own; does a season ever carry `audienceRating`, `Rating`, `Guid`, `Genre`, `Director`, `Writer`, `Role`, `Collection`, `Label`, `Image`, `UltraBlurColors`, `Field`? Report every attribute whose presence rule on real data differs from plexsim's, with the counts behind it.

**Part 2 -- every listing rule, re-asked at `type=3` and `type=4`.** The filter grammar, the sort keys, `group=`, `limit=`, the secondary axes, `firstCharacter`, `/search`, `/autocomplete`, `/query/parse`, `duplicate=`, the leaf-count filters, `id=`, `guid=`, `file=`, `parent=` -- each of these was measured on a MOVIE library or on a show library at `type=2`. Re-ask each at `type=3` and `type=4` against zen and kids, and report where the answer differs from the rule this project recorded. Where the rule involves a column most rows have no value for, say how many rows had none: that is exactly the shape cycle 19's four filter rules came out of.

**Part 3 -- the media column family on real leaves.** Cycle 19 measured that `sort=duration` and `duration=` read the MEDIA's duration, that a show or season listing reads a media column from its LEAVES and matches when ANY leaf does, and left `audioLanguage`'s six non-`=` operators unexplained (zen: `==en` 1,512, `!==en` 528, `<=en` 1,330, `>=en` 148, `<<=en` 3,664, `>>=en` 657). Take the whole media-column family at `type=3`/`type=4` -- `duration`, `resolution`, `hdr`, `dovi`, `mediaSize`, `file`, `audioLanguage`, `subtitleLanguage`, `audioChannels`, `videoCodec`/`audioCodec`/`subtitleCodec`/`audioLayout` where the version advertises them, `unmatched`, `duplicate`, `hasChapters`/`hasMarkers` if they exist -- and for each: which operators parse, what a row with no value answers, what a PARENT row answers, and whether a model fits `audioLanguage`'s six. A fitted model must be HELD OUT against queries it was not fitted to before you report it (HANDOVER 10, "Fit a model, then HOLD OUT").

**Part 4 -- the season and episode subsets, and deep paging.** `/library/sections/2/onDeck`, `/recentlyAdded`, `/newest`, `/recentlyViewed`, `/recentlyViewedShows`, `/allLeaves` on a show and on a season, `/children` at every offset, `/grandchildren`, `/hubs/sections/2`, `/hubs/metadata/{season}/related` and `/postplay`, `/library/recentlyAdded?type=3`/`4`, and the server-wide `/library/onDeck`, each against a library with 507 shows of real watch state. What does each container carry, what does each ROW carry, how does paging behave past the first page and past the last row, and where does plexsim differ? zen has real per-show watch state; kids has almost none, so where they disagree say which is the content difference and which is the rule.

## Report format

Most important first. For each finding: the exact request (method, path, query params in order, the headers that matter); each reference's verbatim response (trimmed but real), naming WHICH servers you measured it on; plexsim's verbatim response; **what it costs a real client** (what breaks, and for whom) -- a finding that changes nothing any client can see should say so; and which of the six failure classes in HANDOVER.md 3.1 your own measurement could belong to, and why it does not. Then how many requests/paths you triaged as (a) catalog content or a version difference, (b) already recorded above, (c) a real behavioural difference -- report only (c) as findings. **If you find nothing in (c), say exactly "no findings"** and list what you compared. End with the two snapshot comparison results.

Cycle 14, measured by the driver BEFORE this hunt and being implemented right now -- do not re-report: (a) the light row's `Director` cap is **3** and `Producer` is absent from a light row entirely (measured on zen across movie, show and episode rows; kids has no row rich enough); (b) `PUT /library/metadata/{id}/unmatch` on a SEASON CASCADES to every episode under it -- each episode's guid becomes `local://<id>`, its title the generated `Episode N`, its `parentGuid` follows the season to `local://<season id>`, its `summary` is emptied, its `year`, `audienceRating` and people tags are dropped, its `titleSort` is removed and its `parentThumb` becomes null -- while the season itself loses its `year` (gaining `parentYear` from the show), falls its `thumb` back to the SHOW's thumb and keeps its own `parentGuid` and external `Guid` array; (c) the `<CODEC> External` sidecar-subtitle label, now confirmed on zen as well as the copy.

Cycle 13 additions — do not re-report (24 commits, all on `main` at 5a07a1c): a row with NO value sorts BELOW every value on every sort key and row type (twelve of the fifteen advertised sorts default to `desc`); a light row caps `Director` at THREE and carries no `Producer` at all; `/hubs/search` returns the ITEMS behind a matched tag, each naming it in `reason`/`reasonID`/`reasonTitle`, while `/library/search` carries the tag rows and title hits only; the seventeen search hubs' base ORDER (`movie` fifth, `collection` fourteenth); the "All episodes" row follows the PAGE's row count, not the offset — present whenever a page returns more than one season row, wherever it starts, gone on a one-row page even at `Start=0`; `duplicate=1` is "more than one Media", and on a SHOW or SEASON "a leaf has more than one"; a bare `<field>=` means three different things (a tag-TABLE field matches NOTHING, `studio`/`editionTitle` match EVERY row, everything else matches the rows with no value) and `contentRating=None` in that spelling is the empty synonym and the key of that axis's trailing bucket; a tag or field axis REFUSES an unparseable `type=` (500, or 400 on `year`) where a MEDIA axis ignores `type=` completely; an EPISODE with an `audienceRating` carries `audienceRatingImage` and a `Rating` row and a SEASON never does; an episode carries its SHOW's `contentRating`, and editing the show's moves it onto the episodes that have none of their own; a show's `/allLeaves` — and no other list — stamps the show's `studio` on every episode row; a season row of `/library/recentlyAdded` alone carries `parentSummary`; `viewedLeafCount`, `viewedOrInProgressLeafCount` and `unviewedLeafCount` filter a show listing although no Meta advertises them; a shelf with no `count=` carries SIX rows and says `more`; 1.42.2 builds three shelves 1.43.4 does not (`movie.topunwatched`, `tv.startwatching`, `tv.toprated`); `pinnedContentDirectoryID` reshapes the promoted Recently Added shelf into `home.movies.recent.N` on 1.42.2 and changes nothing on 1.43.4; unmatching a SEASON unmatches every episode under it and unmatching a SHOW takes its seasons AND episodes, with every descendant's `parentGuid`/`grandparentGuid` following; an episode unmatch drops the people lists, `audienceRating` and `year` and empties `summary` by either path; an unmatch leaves NO activity running; a season's `index` lands on its episodes' `parentIndex`.

Cycle 15 (e1f5a42) -- cycle 14's own 29, all on `main`: `titleSort` is the title TRIMMED and combining-mark FOLDED and then article-stripped (`Amelie` for `Amelie`), and the word matchers (`/hubs/search`, `/library/search`) FOLD unconditionally while the substring box, `/autocomplete` and `title!=` do not -- `title=` on a listing folds only while the query holds NO SPACE; `/filters` and the `Meta` descriptor are PER VERSION (1.42.2, the build plexsim reports, advertises 22 movie filters, 9 at `type=4` and 5 at `type=10`; 1.43.4 advertises 28/17/6, the difference being the media-codec axes plus `hdr`/`dovi` on the episode list); a `group=`'s surviving row is the group's LOWEST `addedAt`, picked BEFORE the sort, so the same SET comes back under every sort, and a type-prefixed `group=` key is SCOPED like a filter; the `actor` axis is the union of each item's FIRST THREE roles (not every Role tag); nine columns filter that no `Meta` advertises (`viewOffset`, `skipCount`, `ratingCount`, `absoluteIndex`, `lastRatedAt`, `updatedAt`, `createdAt`, `mediaCount`, `originalTitle`), `parentIndex` is a 500 on a listing and a MOVIE row's `index` column is 1 on every row; thirteen more `sort=` keys work than `includeAdvanced=1` advertises (`guid`, `originalTitle`, `contentRatingAge`, `studio`, `skipCount`, `absoluteIndex`, `mediaSize`, `createdAt`, `type`, `viewedLeafCount`, `tagline`, `summary`, `editionTitle`) while `leafCount`, `childCount`, `slug` and `key` are 500s; `titleSort`/`summary`/`tagline`/`originalTitle` take the ORDERING keys because their six-operator restriction is the ADVERTISED FieldType's; `/status/sessions/history/all` VALIDATES `accountID`/`librarySectionID`/`metadataItemID` (400 on a non-integer, 404 on a `metadataItemID` naming no row, `0` included, an unknown `librarySectionID` an empty 200, `deviceID` not validated at all) and `viewedAt>=` refuses a non-integer; `group=` reaches the section `allLeaves` while an ITEM's `/allLeaves?group=` is ignored; `#` is the DIGITS bucket of `firstCharacter` and any other non-letter gets a bucket of its OWN listed after Z, every bucket key percent-encoded; `/library/sections/{id}/userRating` is a real axis with a FIXED six-bucket scale (`10` "5 stars" down to `2` "1 star" plus `-1` "Not rated") that IGNORES `type=`; the nine axes of OTHER library types (`mood`, `style`, `format`, `subformat`, `source`, `make`, `model`, `place`, `tag`) answer an EMPTY secondary container with their own `title2` rather than a 404, and they VALIDATE `type=` the way `/genre` does; `/library/metadata/{id}/banners` and `/thumbs` are real candidate lists (200 on both versions) while `/squareArts` is 1.43.4-only; `/library/sections/{id}/recentlyReleased`, `/library/metadata/{id}/background` and `/library/metadata/{id}/composite` are 404s on both references and plexsim had INVENTED all three; On Deck is the MOST RECENTLY VIEWED in-progress episode, not the lowest-numbered; an artwork PICK re-versions `thumb`, `art` and `theme` onto ONE stamp, moves `updatedAt`, LOCKS the picked field, pushes one frame (state 5, no `metadataState`) and CASCADES the stamp to the children that borrow it; a hub container says `allowSync: true` on every hub path except `/hubs/metadata/{id}/postplay`, which says false, while `/hubs/search` and `/hubs/items` carry none; on a SEASON listing the leaf-count filters read the SHOW's counts; a collection answers `/children` on the METADATA path as a full secondary container (with `key`, `parentIndex` = its TAG id, `parentTitle`, `title2`, `nocache` and the librarySection triple) while `/library/collections/{id}/children` answers the BARE `{size: N}`, a `type=18` listing is a real QUERY, a collection row's `index` is its own tag id, and `/library/all?type=18` lists every collection the server holds across libraries in title order; the prefs cascade belongs to `showOrdering` ALONE (45 frames) while `flattenSeasons`, `episodeSort`, `useOriginalTitle` and `subtitleMode` push ZERO frames and change the ROWS instead (`flattenSeasons=1` sets `skipChildren` on the show and `skipParent` on every episode while REMOVING their `parentRatingKey`/`parentKey`; `episodeSort=1` puts a season's `/children` newest first while `/allLeaves` keeps broadcast order); a merge whose TARGET has no `Media` of its own (a season, a show) absorbs NOTHING -- a 200, no frames, the tree unchanged -- and the `split` after it is a no-op too.

Cycle 15, measured by the DRIVER before this hunt and being implemented right now -- do not re-report: (a) `titleSort`'s whitespace trim is LEFT-ONLY, not `.strip()` -- a title written with a trailing space keeps it in `titleSort` (`'  Zeta Both  '` sorts as `'Zeta Both  '`, `'The Zeta Art '` as `'Zeta Art '`) while leading whitespace goes; (b) the SECTION-level `flattenSeasons`/`episodeSort` produce the SAME row effects as the item path, and the ITEM pref is a tri-state where `-1` means INHERIT the section's value and `0`/`1` override it in both directions; (c) the `group=` `addedAt` tie-break is the LOWEST ratingKey and is sort-independent (settled on the copy, where movies 1 and 17 share `addedAt` 1789187071) -- plexsim was already right.

Cycle 16 (49 findings, all landed on `main` -- **do not re-report any of these**):
`/library/sections/{id}/firstCharacter` counts a row BELOW the library's top-level type by its SHOW's sort title while the LISTING behind each bucket filters on the row's OWN stored sort title, so a season list's `A` bucket can say 64 and answer 0 rows; `/firstCharacter/{key}` is a real listing (`title2` = `Start with '<key>'`, the key echoed as sent, `%23` decoded, the light grid row, the whole listing grammar on top); **every** section leaf's extra path segment is that leaf's BUCKET or a 404 and never the leaf again (`/all/zzz`, `/onDeck/zzz`, `/sorts/zzz`, `/studio/<key>`, `/audioLanguage/en`, `/location/1` and fourteen more are 404s; a tag axis is keyed by TAG ID, `year`/`decade` by a non-negative integer with `decade/1995` a ten-year window, `contentRating` as `Rated <v>`, `resolution` accepting anything, `userRating` as `<n> Star` refusing `-1`); a show library's `?type=3`/`?type=4` grid defaults to BROADCAST order (show sort title, `parentIndex`, `index`); a show library's `/unwatched` carries `nocache`; an artwork path is a 200 only on the row that OWNS the image and a borrower carries the owner's path verbatim.
A real PMS LOCALISES its own text: `X-Plex-Language` (header or query) beats `Accept-Language`, a regional form works like its primary subtag, an unknown language falls back to English, 28 languages answer differently, and the filter/sort menus, `Meta` type titles, hub titles, a GENERATED `Season N`, a stream's language name and `displayTitle` and all 200+ `/:/prefs` strings move while the LIBRARY's own rows, `All Movies`, an axis's `title2` and `Unplayed` do not -- the table is keyed by ATTRIBUTE and then by the English string (`translations.json`).
A real PMS ROUTES before it authenticates: with no token an unknown path is a 404, exactly eleven single-segment paths are 401 (`library hubs playlists devices butler servers clients accounts activities playQueues log`), `/identity` and `/security` are 200 and every other one is a 403; `GET /` is a 401 with a 193-byte body that walks a browser into the web client.
Every response names its connection state (`Connection: Keep-Alive` + `Keep-Alive: timeout=20`, and an idle socket really is closed at 20 s); a paged container answers `X-Plex-Container-Start`/`-Total-Size` as HEADERS and the paging HEADER beats the query per parameter (a header `Start=abc` beside a valid query is a 500); `Range` applies to EVERY response body (206/416, an unrecognised unit read as bytes, a 206 never gzipped); a malformed request header is a 400 (no colon, a TAB in a value, a name over 1024, a value over 4096); the Accept `q` grammar RANKS a zero rather than dropping it (a range qualifies only if its type is exactly `application/json`/`application/xml` and it has no parameters or a first parameter beginning exactly `q=`; no parameter weighs 1.0, a single parseable non-negative q weighs that, everything else weighs 0 and still qualifies); `Accept-Encoding` tokens match exactly and case-sensitively (`GZIP`, `x-gzip` and `*` get plain; `gzip;q=0` is still gzipped); no `100 Continue`; a preflight with no `Origin` carries no CORS headers; `/web` and `/web/` are a `302 Moved Temporarily` with an ABSOLUTE Location from the request's own `Host` and `Cache-Control: public` (the one non-`no-cache` place on the surface) while every `/web/*` asset is `no-cache`, `Accept-Ranges: bytes`, never gzipped, `.js` is `text/javascript`, `.html` carries no charset and `.ico` is `application/octet-stream`; a section's bare `/composite` is a 404 and only `/composite/{version}` answers; `X-Plex-Platform: Roku` strips `id` and `Pivot` from every library `Directory` of `/media/providers`; a `Meta/Type` orders its children `Filter, Sort, Field`, `includeDetails=1` carries no `active`/`activeDirection` on a Sort nor `advanced` on a Filter where `includeMeta=1` carries all three, the rows `includeChildren=1` inlines carry no librarySection triple, and a MediaContainer's attributes come out `size`, then `totalSize`/`offset` when paged, then alphabetically; `POST /library/metadata/{id}/extras` is a 400 whatever the body; **a real PMS sends no `Server:` banner at all**.
`/playlists/{id}/items` is a real typed QUERY (`type=2` answers the SHOW owning the member episodes, `type=3` their seasons, `-1` everything, `1.5` a 400; the library filters apply; `sort=`/`limit=` are parsed then ignored and an unparseable one is a 500) and `?includeMeta=1` carries the page's own tab bar; a playlist is born `viewCount: 1` with `lastViewedAt == addedAt` and the pair is on the LISTING row only; `/playlists` filters by `title`, `smart`, `sectionID` and `type` (15; `type=abc` is a 500) and its default order is SMART first then `titleSort`; `sort=mediaCount` is real there and `leafCount` a 500; `POST /playlists/{id}/items` is a 404 (only PUT adds); an empty `title=` is stored verbatim; `smart=1` over a comma-joined KEY LIST is a 400 (the test is the uri's SHAPE, never whether it resolves); a uri the server cannot resolve adds NOTHING; `PUT /playlists/{id}` takes the whole ITEM edit grammar (thumb/art mint artwork under `/library/metadata/{playlist ratingKey}/`, every `.locked` locks, `year`/`originallyAvailableAt`/`studio` store, `userRating`/`guid`/`duration`/timestamps are accepted and discarded); `playlistItemID` is a SERVER-WIDE sequence and a smart list's items carry none (every item write on one is a 400); a playlist's artwork lives under `/library/metadata/{ratingKey}/` (posters, arts, thumbs, banners, tree all 200) while `/playlists/{id}/{posters,arts,thumb,art}` and the bare `composite` are 404s.
A shuffled play queue is really SHUFFLED (plexsim reversed it deterministically); `includeBefore`/`includeAfter` window the queue with NO `window=` (unlimited radius) and each is a boolean whose every other value is a 400; EVERY item delete answers the container as it stood BEFORE it and only the SELECTED one also drops the rows; a move refuses `after=<itself>` (404) and an empty `after=` (400); a queue built from a PLAYLIST uri carries `playQueuePlaylistID`/`playQueuePlaylistTitle` and no `playQueueSourceURI`.
The SECTION save path stores an unlocked `titleSort.value=` until the next TITLE write re-derives it (where the ITEM path discards it); a show's `contentRating` reaches only the episodes carrying none of their own; a show's `index` cascades onto its seasons' `parentIndex`; a season that LOSES its year gains `parentYear` in the same read; `type=abc` on the save path is a 400; an UNMATCHED item drops its agent-driven prefs (a show loses `languageOverride` and `useOriginalTitle`, a MOVIE loses the whole `Preferences` block, a season keeps its three); and on the section save path the `state 3` frame carries the row's title from BEFORE the write while `state 5` carries the new one.

Cycle 17 (29 findings, all landed on `main` at b3e3ea7 -- **do not re-report any of these**):
Three fixtures were captured off the WRONG VERSION and are now split per version: `Meta.Type[].Field` (1.42.2 advertises 34 movie Fields and 20 episode ones against 1.43.4's 40 and 26, differing in exactly the six media-codec axes `hdr10plus`/`atmos`/`videoCodec`/`audioCodec`/`subtitleCodec`/`audioLayout`), and those six are UNKNOWN fields on 1.42.2 so a listing filtered on one answers EVERY row and `/query/parse` reports an `Error`; `system_agents.json` (`tv.plex.agents.nfo.movie` and `.series` are 1.43.4-only -- 1.42.2 lists four modern agents and two per media type); and `translations.json`, widened from 342 to 451 entries over ~100 paths (the whole library and item PREFERENCES family's `label`/`summary`/`enumValues`, the seventeen search hubs, the butler's task titles and descriptions, a generated `Episode N`, `/query/parse`'s `fieldTitle`).
The localiser reaches INSIDE a row for exactly two blocks: the `Preferences` block's `label`/`summary`/`enumValues` and a `Stream`'s language fields -- where the two display titles are COMPOSED from the localised name and the omission is decided on the ENGLISH one -- while the row's own title, summary and tags stay English.
A real PMS escapes XML attribute values into pure ASCII: `&amp;`/`&lt;`/`&gt;` named, `"` and `'` NUMERIC (`&#34;`, `&#39;`), TAB decimal `&#9;` but NEWLINE and CR HEX (`&#xA;`, `&#xD;`), every codepoint from U+0080 up its DECIMAL reference (an emoji is one `&#128512;`), every control XML 1.0 forbids a literal `?`; the surface carries no element TEXT.
A ROW's XML attribute ORDER is measured -- 25 element buckets in `xmlout._ATTR_ORDER`, a topological sort of every PAIR both references emit, 634 inverted pairs fixed and 0 left; a tag is `id, filter, tag`, a SORT row `default, defaultDirection, descKey, firstCharacterKey, key, title`, a `firstCharacter` bucket `size, key, title`.
`titleSort` strips a leading run of PUNCTUATION as well as whitespace, before the article: in ASCII only `" ' ( - . [ { ~`, and outside it every codepoint from U+0080..U+00BF and U+2000..U+2FFF, while U+0100..U+1FFF and U+3000 up stay; the run may mix with whitespace and repeat, and a title made of nothing else keeps its own text.
A show's `/allLeaves` -- and no other container -- stamps a leaf's BORROWED artwork (`grandparentThumb`, `grandparentArt`, `grandparentTheme`, `art`) with the LEAF's own `addedAt`; everywhere else they carry the show's current version. A property of the LIST, not of the row.
A save a real PMS REFUSES writes NOTHING (validate the whole form before applying any of it); the ITEM edit path's 400 means "this request edits nothing" -- with a real edit beside it every unrecognised name is IGNORED and each alone is a 400 -- while the SECTION path answers 200 to a form that writes nothing and still 400s an unknown `.value`.
A collection's `/children` on the METADATA path is the LIGHT grid row with the librarySection triple on the CONTAINER, `allowSync: true` and no `thumb`; the collection ITEM paths take one verb each (`/items` PUT plus GET/HEAD, `/items/{rk}` DELETE, `/move` PUT) and 404 the rest, a non-numeric ratingKey on the DELETE is a 404 where an unknown number is a 200, and the MOVE refuses `after=<itself>`/empty/unparseable with a 400, sends an `after=` naming no member to the FRONT and works through the `/children/{rk}/move` alias; a comma-joined add TOLERATES the members it cannot use and only a single-key uri of the wrong type is a 400.
A LOCAL artwork candidate names a server path: `ratingKey` is the bare uri and `key`/`thumb` are `/library/metadata/{id}/file?url=<encoded, `.` as `%2E`>` which really serves the image; a `media://` or `upload://` candidate carries no `provider`; `DELETE .../thumb` clears the poster like `/poster`; an artwork UPLOAD moves `updatedAt` onto the new stamp exactly as a PICK does.
A library PREFERENCE write moves the SECTION's `updatedAt`; an INT pref that does not parse is stored as 0 (not ignored); a TEXT pref is stored percent-escaped for `:` and `|` (lowercase hex) with a value carrying `%` stored EMPTY.
Twelve butler tasks report a different sentence than plexsim did, three report SEVERAL and five report one where it reported none; `CleanOldBundles` is titled `Cleaning Bundles` and steps its activity SUBTITLE through four named phases, `OptimizeDatabase` is `Optimizing database`/`Optimizing Database`; and an ACTIVITY's subtitle is the row's FRAME title (a season's `<show> S01`, an episode's `<show> S01 E01`), not `title (year)`.
`POST` on an item ACTION leaf is a 404 (`/refresh`, `/analyze`, `/unmatch` take PUT) while `POST /library/sections/{id}/refresh` is a 200; `/match` answers EVERY verb with its no-parameters 400; `?force=` on a section refresh is PARSED (`abc`, `1.5`, empty are 400s); a KNOWN single-segment path (`/butler`, `/devices`) falls through to the legacy plug-in dispatcher for the verbs it does not implement; and `GET /livetv` is that dispatcher's three-row container (`dvrs`, `epg`, `sessions`, `content="plugins"`), not an empty one.

Cycle 18 (26 findings, all landed on `main` at b3e3ea7 -- **do not re-report any of these**):
A title the SERVER generated is stored NOWHERE: a season's `Season N` and an unmatched episode's `Episode N` have an EMPTY stored sort title (`titleSort=` answers 740 of zen's 741 seasons and 4,630 of its 9,287 episodes, `titleSort<<=0` the same set, every LETTER bucket of a season `firstCharacter` listing answers none while `#` answers them all), and it is in no search index either (`search?type=3&query=season` answers nothing until the season is renamed; zen's `type=3&title=Season` is 0 of 741). An agent-supplied `Season 1`/`Episode 1` IS stored and nothing on the row tells the two apart.
Eleven Latin-1 characters fold in `titleSort` that NFD cannot reach, swept character by character over U+0080..U+02FF: `AE->E D->D x->x O->0 P->P ss->s ae->e eth->O div->/ oslash->0 thorn->p` (`O`/`o` slash become the DIGIT ZERO and eth a capital `O`). Nothing above U+02FF folds.
The bare `title=` matcher also reads the row's ORIGINAL title, and only it does: `title=the` is 1,484 of zen's 3,887 where the title alone explains 1,481. `title!=`, `==`, `!==`, `<=` and `>=` read the title alone.
On an UNLISTED string field (`titleSort`, `summary`, `tagline`, `originalTitle`) the four ordering keys really ORDER: `<=` is `<<=` and `>=` is `>>=`, case-insensitive lexicographic, both STRICT. They are begins-with/ends-with only on an ADVERTISED string field.
`/library/sections/{id}/search?type=N` types its container from the ASKED type, not the library: `type=3` is `viewGroup=season` and `viewMode` 65593, `type=4` is `viewGroup=episode` with `mixedParents`, and a MOVIE library answers both. The section GRID keeps the library's own type.
`All <library title>` on a movie library and the constant `All Shows` on a show one, in both the `/all` container's `title2` and the `all` pivot row. Not localised.
Three endpoints, three meanings for `limit=`: `/library/recentlyAdded?limit=N` caps AND rewrites `totalSize` (0 none, negative lifts the 50-row cap, unparseable ignored); `/status/sessions/history/all` caps and `-1` answers NONE; a section listing's `limit=0` answers ONE row and an unparseable one is a 500. `group=` on the history page is a 400.
`parentIndex=` and an out-of-enum `resolution=` refuse on `/library/all` and `/playlists` as well as on a section listing.
`/hubs`, and a SHOW library's `/hubs/sections/{id}`, 500 fifteen filter keys (`duration lastViewedAt lastRatedAt viewCount skipCount viewOffset userRating unwatched resolution hdr mediaSize file parentIndex slug group`) for any non-empty value and whatever operator; an EMPTY value and an unknown field are 200s, and a MOVIE library's hub page takes all fifteen.
`/system` PAGES (`totalSize` FIRST, no `offset`, and the response headers) where `/system/scanners/{N}` ignores paging; the plug-in framework's container is ordered `[totalSize,] noHistory, replaceParent, size, identifier`; `/system/search` is a real empty container carrying `prefsKey`; and the whole `/system` subtree has its own envelope -- PATCH is a 405 (the only one on the surface), a bare OPTIONS an empty 200, every other verb an EMPTY-bodied 404.
Four more single-segment paths are the plug-in dispatcher's POPULATED listings (`/media`, `/services`, `/status`, `/statistics`) and `/security` is a real empty container with NO `content` key; a KNOWN single-segment path answers the dispatcher for every verb it does not implement, `PUT /identity` and `PUT /hubs` included.
The playlist item paths take one verb each: `/playlists/{id}` and `/items` GET/HEAD/PUT/DELETE, `/items/{iid}` DELETE (PUT is a 400, a non-numeric id a 404), `/items/{iid}/move` PUT. `DELETE /library/metadata/{playlist}` is an empty 200 that deletes NOTHING where `DELETE /playlists/{id}` is a 204 that does.
A repeated `id=` on the section save path edits nothing (the comma list edits both); `POST` on `/split` and `/merge` is a 404; a write to `/library/parts/{id}` resolves the part FIRST (an unknown or malformed id is a 404 whatever the parameters); `/subtitles` answers 404 to DELETE, PATCH and OPTIONS.
A library DELETE pushes one `state 9 deleted` frame per row -- `type: 12`, an EMPTY title, `sectionID` the NEGATIVE section id -- between the 10% and 40% activity updates.
`section_prefs_new.json` is per VERSION too (the fourth such fixture): on 1.42.2 the AGENT's settings are appended whatever the `type=`, and 1.43.4 adds five generic rows to `tv.plex.agents.none` at types 1 and 2.
An item's `audioLanguage`/`subtitleLanguage` enum is led by the ITEM's own stream languages (bare 639-1 tags, audio first then subtitles, each named in its own language) before the generic regional list.

Cycle 19 (31 findings, all landed on `main` at b3e3ea7 -- **do not re-report any of these**):
A BOOLEAN filter answers the true set to exactly two requests -- `field=1` and `field!=0`; every other operator and every other value answers the rows where the column is FALSE (zen's `unwatched=1` is 3,866 of 3,887 and `!=1`, `==1`, `<=1`, `>=1`, `<<=1`, `>>=1`, `=0`, `==0`, `!==0`, `=2`, `!=2`, `=`, `=01`, `=true` are all the same 21).
A row with NO value for a numeric column matches NOTHING, `!=` and `!==` included (zen's 742 rated movies split 368/374 across `rating<=7` and `rating>>=7`, so the 3,145 unrated are in neither); and `==`/`!==` there are an exact STRING comparison of the stored number (`rating==7` is 0 where `rating==7.0` is 6).
A `tag`-typed COLUMN takes all eight operators where a tag-TABLE field takes two: `contentRating`'s ordering keys are a BYTE-ordered comparison over the stored string, an absent value compares as `""`, `=` is case-SENSITIVE and `==` is not. `resolution` is the opposite -- every operator answers the `=` set, and it matches ANY of a row's Media.
The section `Meta`'s `active` follows the REQUEST, not the library (`?type=3` marks `season`, `?type=4` marks `episode`); `?includeDetails=1` honours `includeAdvanced=1` (9 sorts bare, 15 with it), carries no `folder` Type row and marks none active.
A row that HAS children names its child list -- `key="/library/metadata/{id}/children"` on every show and season of both references, where an episode keeps the bare item path.
`excludeFields=` strips the field from every CHILD ELEMENT (`id` off Media/Part/Stream/Genre/Guid, `file` off Part, `url` off Image, `value` off Rating); `excludeElements=Image` and `=UltraBlurColors` are NO-OPS on both references; `checkFiles=` is validated (`0`, `1`, `01` pass, everything else is a 400).
`/library/metadata/{id}/extras/all` is a real leaf, the same container `/extras` answers; only `/extras/{n}` is the 404.
Four more XML ordering rules: a `Meta`'s `Type` rows precede its `FieldType` rows; `Common`, `Feature` (`scrobbleKey` first), `Type` and the `/media/providers` library `Directory` are element buckets of their own; the rows of `/library/recentlyAdded` and `/library/onDeck` LEAD with `allowSync` and the librarySection triple; a LIGHT row puts `Guid` directly after `UltraBlurColors`, before its tags, where a detail row puts it after `Genre`/`Country`.
A real PMS's JSON key order IS its XML order -- the container's, the row's, `Media`'s, `Part`'s and the row's child-element keys. `xmlout._split` is now the one answer both writers ask.
On a SHOW or SEASON listing a media column is read from the LEAVES and matches when ANY leaf does (zen's `duration<=1200000` and `>=1200000` answer 39 and 483 of 505).
The default listing order IS `sort=titleSort` and every tie falls back to `titleSort` ASCENDING, in both directions -- re-measured against zen's own ordering of 3,887 real rows.
A library EDIT scans only when the form carries `language` or `scanner` (`agent` alone, with `name`, or with a `prefs[...]` is a 200 with ZERO frames) and it REFUSES a `type` naming a different type from the library's own. `?path=` SCOPES a scan: the section's own Location, an empty value and no path scan everything; a parent of it, a child of it and a path it does not hold scan nothing.
Three butler tasks push the wrong thing: `RefreshLibraries` runs a section SCAN per library; `OptimizeDatabase` starts at 0 with an EMPTY subtitle and steps 0/10/40/60 to an `ended` at 100; `CleanOldBundles` has SEVEN updates ending on two `Deleting N metadata bundles` steps at 25 and 50.
A show library's scan names the SHOW before its seasons, in a `progress` row with no activity update of its own; `provider.subscriptions.process` is the one `cancellable: true` activity; an analyze pushes NO `backgroundProcessingQueue` frame; `PUT /updater/check` pushes one `update.statechange` frame carrying an `AutoUpdateNotification` row.
`DELETE /devices/{id}` and `DELETE /accounts/{id}` are 404s for an id the server lists and one it does not; `PUT /activities/{x}` is a 400 where its DELETE is a 404; `HEAD /:/prefs` is a 200.
Four settings answer 403 to a PUT (`PlexOnlineUrl`, `TranscoderH264Options`, `TranscoderH264OptionsOverride`, `allowMediaDeletionLanOnly`) even when the value written is the one already stored, while `ButlerStartHour` and `ButlerEndHour` take a WORD with a 200 that stores nothing.
`server_prefs.json` is the FIFTH per-version fixture: `AllowLegacyAgents` and `CLDriver` are 1.43.4-only, and a write to one is refused as an unknown preference.
The Add Library form refuses a missing or unlisted `language` (the agent's own list, case-insensitive), accepts an EMPTY `location` and accepts a NUMERIC `type`, creating a library of that type.
`filters=` on a notification channel is a WHITELIST and `all` is not an event type, so a socket opened with `filters=all` receives NOTHING. Always open with a control write.

## Your territory: the EDIT grammar swept by FIELD and by ROW TYPE -- what a write to each column actually does

Cycle 4 measured the Edit dialog, cycles 9-12 the write cascades and the frames, cycle 17 the write surface below the playlist, cycle 18 below the collection item, and cycle 19 the write's own PAYLOAD (the frame, the `Activity`, the server-level writers). **Nobody has ever swept the EDIT itself by COLUMN.** Every edit rule this project holds was learnt from whichever field a bug happened to touch: `title`, `titleSort`, `summary`, `year`, `originalTitle`, `contentRating`, `studio`, `userRating`, `originallyAvailableAt`, the tag fields and the `.locked` flag. The grammar is recorded as if those were the whole surface. They are not.

Your instrument is **plexscratch** (`http://100.85.222.1:32410`, token `$SCRATCH_TOKEN`) -- a disposable, isolated copy you may write to freely, resettable with `ssh ben@kids /home/ben/plexscratch/scratch.sh reset` (the token CHANGES on every reset, so re-read it). kids and zen are **GET/HEAD only**.

Do this in four parts and report each part's coverage numbers whether or not it produced a finding.

**Part 1 -- the column census.** Enumerate every column a row of each type actually carries (read a real row of each of movie, show, season, episode, collection, playlist on the copy and on kids), and then, for EACH column and EACH row type, send a write of it on the ITEM path (`PUT /library/metadata/{id}?<field>.value=<v>&<field>.locked=1`) and on the SECTION path (`PUT /library/sections/{id}/all?type=N&id={id}&<field>.value=<v>`). For each (column x row type x path) record: the status; whether the value is STORED, IGNORED, TRANSFORMED or REFUSED; whether `.locked` is honoured and whether the column appears in the `Field` list; what frames it pushes; and whether it CASCADES to children or to a parent. Report every cell where plexsim answers differently -- and state the size of the grid you covered. Columns this project has never written: `index`, `parentIndex`, `absoluteIndex`, `duration`, `addedAt`, `updatedAt`, `createdAt`, `lastViewedAt`, `viewCount`, `skipCount`, `viewOffset`, `ratingCount`, `audienceRating`, `rating`, `tagline`, `slug`, `guid`, `editionTitle`, `chapterSource`, `languageOverride`, `originalTitle` on a season, `contentRating` on a season, `theme`, `banner`, `composite`, `sortTitle` spelt any other way. Send each one; an unknown field's refusal is a rule too.

**Part 2 -- the value grammar per TYPE of column.** For the columns that DO store, sweep the value space: empty, whitespace, a negative number, a float where an int is expected, a number where a string is expected, a string where a number is expected, a date in five spellings, a value at the column's apparent limit, a value with `%`, `|`, `:`, a newline, a tab, a control character, an emoji, 10 KB of text, and the same value written twice. ## Your territory: the CLIENT-IDENTITY dimension -- what a real PMS answers differently to a different client

Every dimension this programme has swept mechanically is now closed: path (cycle 14), method and parameter (15), request and response headers (16), the row (17), the container (18), the child element (19), the filter engine against a real library (18-20), attribute order (22) and child-element order (23). **This is a dimension nobody has swept at all.**

plexsim knows of exactly TWO client-identity rules, both found by accident:

1. `X-Plex-Platform: Roku` -- that exact spelling -- strips `id` and `Pivot` from every library `Directory` of `/media/providers` and changes nothing else.
2. Thirteen products (Plex Web; Plex for iOS, Apple TV, Android (TV), Android (Mobile), LG, Samsung, Windows, Mac, Xbox, PlayStation 4, PlayStation 5, Vizio -- exact names, header or query) get ` (<CODEC>)` appended to a video's and a subtitle's `displayTitle`. plexsim adds it on the way out in `_with_client_titles`.

Two rules cannot be the whole of it. Every official Plex client sends a full identity block on every request -- `X-Plex-Product`, `X-Plex-Version`, `X-Plex-Client-Identifier`, `X-Plex-Platform`, `X-Plex-Platform-Version`, `X-Plex-Device`, `X-Plex-Device-Name`, `X-Plex-Model`, `X-Plex-Features`, `X-Plex-Provides`, `X-Plex-Session-Identifier`, `X-Plex-Language`, `X-Plex-Drm`, `X-Plex-Text-Format`, `X-Plex-Client-Profile-Extra`, `X-Plex-Client-Profile-Name` -- and a real PMS reads them. **Your job is to find every place the answer moves, and to say which header moved it.**

How to run it, as a sweep and not as a guess:

1. **Build a matrix.** Pick ~12 real client identities (Plex Web on Chrome; Plex for iOS; Plex for Android (Mobile) and (TV); Plex for Apple TV; Plex for Roku; Plex for Samsung; Plex for LG; Plex for Xbox One; Plex for PlayStation 5; Plexamp; plex.tv's own `Plex Web` on a different platform; and a bare request with NO identity headers at all). Use the real product and platform STRINGS -- the exact spellings matter, as `Roku` already showed. `/devices` on kids lists every client that has ever connected with its `X-Plex-Device-Name` and `X-Plex-Platform`; read it for real spellings, and check `docs/findings.md` for the thirteen product names already recorded.
2. **Pick ~60 paths** that cover the surface a client actually walks: `/`, `/identity`, `/media/providers`, `/library/sections`, a section grid at each type, `/library/sections/{id}/all` with and without `includeMeta=1`, `/filters`, `/sorts`, a detail row for a movie / show / season / episode, `/children`, `/allLeaves`, `/hubs`, `/hubs/promoted`, `/hubs/home/*`, `/hubs/sections/{id}`, `/hubs/search`, `/library/search`, `/playlists`, a playlist and a collection row, `/:/prefs`, `/system/agents`, `/library/metadata/{id}/tree`, `/status/sessions`, `/updater/status`, `/butler` (GET only), `/clients`, `/devices`, `/accounts`, `/servers`, `/security`, `/system`, an artwork path, `/photo/:/transcode`. **Fetch each path once per identity, on kids AND on zen**, then diff every identity's answer against the Plex-Web baseline.
3. **Report only where BOTH references move the same way.** A difference kids shows and zen does not is a version difference, by construction.
4. **Then check plexsim** on each rule you found, and say what it costs a real client -- which client, on which screen.

Questions the sweep should answer, none of which anybody here has measured:

- Which OTHER paths does `X-Plex-Platform: Roku` reshape, and is the trigger really the platform or is it `X-Plex-Product: Plex for Roku`? Send each half alone.
- Does a client's `X-Plex-Features` or `X-Plex-Client-Profile-Extra` change a Media, Part or Stream row -- `optimizedForStreaming`, `selected`, `Stream.key`, the audio/subtitle stream the server marks selected, `videoProfile`?
- Does `X-Plex-Drm` or `X-Plex-Model` change anything at all?
- Does `X-Plex-Provides: player` / `controller` / `server` change `/clients`, `/devices`, `/status/sessions` or the notification surface?
- Does a missing `X-Plex-Client-Identifier` change a status, a container attribute, or `allowSync`?
- Does `X-Plex-Language` reach anything BEYOND the localiser's measured list (the filter and sort menus, `Meta` titles, hub titles, a generated `Season N`, a Stream's language fields, the `/:/prefs` labels)? Cycle 20 keyed the table by ATTRIBUTE and then by the English string; a path that moves for a client and not for the table is a finding.
- Does the identity change `X-Plex-Protocol`, `X-Plex-Content-*` or any other RESPONSE header?
- Does a TV or console identity change the XML-vs-JSON default, the gzip decision, or the paging defaults?
- Does `X-Plex-Session-Identifier` on a plain read do anything?

Read `gate.py` and `_with_client_titles` in `server.py` before you start, so you know what plexsim already keys on.
